:4	;******  Processing file: E:\github\MoonsweeperC64/MOONCOD1.ASM

1						;--------------------------------
2						; FILE>MOON.CODE.1
3						;--------------------------------
4	.0860	78		sei		COLD_START SEI ; TURN OFF INTERRUPTS
5	.0861	20 4d 0e	jsr $0e4d	JSR INIT_IO ; SETUP I/O HARDWARE
6	.0864	a9 00		lda #$00	LDA #$00
7	.0866	aa		tax		TAX
8	>0867	9d				_1 .byte $9D ; OPCODE FOR STA $ZZZZ,X
9	>0868	05 00				.word TEMP1 ; BYTES FOR ADDRESS OF STA
10	.086a	9d 00 d0	sta $d000,x	STA $D000,X ; CLEAR 6566 VIDEO REGISTERS
11	.086d	9d 00 d4	sta $d400,x	STA $D400,X ; CLEAR 6581 SID REGISTERS
12	.0870	e8		inx		INX
13	.0871	d0 f4		bne $0867	BNE _1
14	.0873	9a		txs		TXS ; RESET STACK POINTER
15	.0874	20 51 0d	jsr $0d51	JSR INIT_HARDWARE
16	.0877	20 88 0e	jsr $0e88	JSR INIT_GAME
17						;--------------------------------
18						; DO THE GALAXY SCREEN
19						;--------------------------------
20	.087a	20 db 0f	jsr $0fdb	GALAXY_LOOP JSR HYPER_SPACE ; DO HYPERSPACE PICTURE
21	.087d	20 f5 0e	jsr $0ef5	JSR MOVE_SCREEN ; PUT IN UNIVERSE
22	.0880	20 66 11	jsr $1166	JSR PICK_GALAXY ; GET GALAXY CHOSEN
23	.0883	20 db 0f	jsr $0fdb	JSR HYPER_SPACE ; DO HYPERSPACE PICTURE
24	.0886	20 f5 0e	jsr $0ef5	JSR MOVE_SCREEN ; PUT IN ORBIT
25						;--------------------------------
26						; DO THE ORBIT SCREEN
27						;--------------------------------
28	.0889	20 a4 08	jsr $08a4	JSR INIT_ORBIT ; SET UP ORBIT SCREEN
29						;--------------------------------
30						; ORBIT LOOP
31						;--------------------------------
32	.088c	20 17 09	jsr $0917	ORBIT_LOOP JSR DO_SHIP ; UPDATE SHIP
33	.088f	20 4a 0c	jsr $0c4a	JSR DO_FUEL ; UPDATE FUEL
34	.0892	20 f2 0c	jsr $0cf2	JSR BLOW_UP_SHIP ; KILL SHIP IF NEEDED
35	.0895	20 8c 0b	jsr $0b8c	JSR UPDATE_SHOT ; UPDATE A SHOT
36	.0898	20 5a 12	jsr $125a	JSR DO_ORBIT ; MOVE ORBIT SPRITES
37	.089b	20 62 14	jsr $1462	JSR COLLISION_DETECT ; CHECK FOR COLLISIONS
38	.089e	20 f9 23	jsr $23f9	JSR DO_SOUNDS
39	.08a1	4c 8c 08	jmp $088c	JMP ORBIT_LOOP ; DO IT AGAIN
40						;--------------------------------
41						; INITIALIZE ORBIT SCENE
42						;--------------------------------
43	.08a4	ad 16 d0	lda $d016	INIT_ORBIT LDA VIC_CONTROL_2 ; TURN ON MULTI COLOR MODE (matches original)
44	.08a7	09 10		ora #$10	ORA #$10 ; SET D016 BIT 4 (charset tiles for planet/orbit are multicolor)

240	.1414	95 d2		sta $d2,x	_09 STA XMOVER,X ; ONLY NEED LOW QUOTIENT
241	.1416	60		rts		RTS
242						;--------------------------------
243						; 16 BY 16 UNSIGNED DIVISION:
244						; DIVIDEND=TEMP1(LOW),TEMP2(HI)
245						; DIVISOR =TEMP3(LOW),TEMP4(HI)
246						; QUOTIENT=REPLACES THE DIVIDEND
247						; REMAINDER=TEMP5(LOW),TEMP6(HI)
248						; DESTROYS X,Y,A
249						;--------------------------------
250	.1417	a9 00		lda #$00	DIVIDER LDA #$00 ; CLEAR PARTIAL DIVIDEND
251	.1419	85 09		sta $09		STA TEMP5
252	.141b	85 0a		sta $0a		STA TEMP6
253	.141d	a2 10		ldx #$10	LDX #$10
254	.141f	06 05		asl $05		NEXT_BIT ASL TEMP1
255	.1421	26 06		rol $06		ROL TEMP2
256	.1423	26 09		rol $09		ROL TEMP5 ; SHIFT PARTIAL DIVIDEND LEFT
257	.1425	26 0a		rol $0a		ROL TEMP6
258	.1427	a5 09		lda $09		LDA TEMP5 ; SUBTRACT LOW BYTES
259	.1429	38		sec		SEC
260	.142a	e5 07		sbc $07		SBC TEMP3
261	.142c	a8		tay		TAY ; SAVE LOW RESULT IN Y
262	.142d	a5 0a		lda $0a		LDA TEMP6 ; SUBTRACT HIGH BYTES
263	.142f	e5 08		sbc $08		SBC TEMP4
264	.1431	90 07		bcc $143a	BCC CNTDN ; DIVISOR>DIVIDEND?
265	.1433	e6 05		inc $05		INC TEMP1 ; NO. SET BIT IN QUOTIENT
266	.1435	85 0a		sta $0a		STA TEMP6 ; AND ENTER SUBTRACTION RESULT
267	.1437	98		tya		TYA
268	.1438	85 09		sta $09		STA TEMP5 ; INTO PARTIAL DIVIDEND.
269	.143a	ca		dex		CNTDN DEX ; BIT COUNT=COUNT-1
270	.143b	d0 e2		bne $141f	BNE NEXT_BIT ; LOOP UNTIL 16 BITS ARE DONE.
271	.143d	60		rts		RTS

:1	;******  Return to file: E:\github\MoonsweeperC64/MOONSWPR.ASM

29						    .include "MOONSPRT.ASM"

:12	;******  Processing file: E:\github\MoonsweeperC64/MOONSPRT.ASM

1						;------------------------------------------------------------------
2						; FILE>MOONSPRT.ASM
3						;
4						; Sprite shape data for the C64 Moonsweeper port.
5						;
6						; The S-C source we converted contains only sprite POINTERS but no
7						; shape data.  The core sprites (ship, shot, saucer, mother, orbit
8						; objects and explosions) below are copied VERBATIM from the
9						; released tape game (MOONSWE1.T64) memory image.
10						;
11						; The gauge needles (VELOCITY/FUEL dials, ptrs $B5-$C4) are also
12						; real tape data: $B5-$C0 come from the tape load image ($6D40-$7000)
13						; and $C2-$C4 from the runtime RAM dump ($7080-$7140) - verified
14						; byte-for-byte against a VICE snapshot of the orbit screen.  Only
15						; the radar blips ($D3/$D4) are reconstructed (not in the tape).
16						;
17						; Blocks are stored compactly and copied to their VIC-bank
18						; destinations by INIT_SPRITES (called from INIT_HARDWARE).
19						;------------------------------------------------------------------

21						; The table sits at $9000 in bank-1 free RAM. It must NOT live in the VIC
22						; bank ($4000-$7FFF): several sprite DESTINATIONS (orbit explode $C9->$7240,
23						; mother $CA->$7280, RING $CB-$D2->$72C0-$7480, radar $74C0/$7500) overlap
24						; this source area, so placing it there would let the copy routines clobber
25						; their own source. A fresh boot's first INIT_SPRITES would still work (each
26						; block is consumed before it's overwritten), but a GAME OVER -> play-again
27						; restart would reload sprites (e.g. the ship bank frames $33-$37) from the
28						; corrupted source. $9000+ is free RAM never touched at runtime, so the source
29						; stays intact across restarts.
30						* = $9000

32						;--------------------------------
33						; COMPACT SPRITE SHAPE DATA (64 bytes per block)
34						;--------------------------------

592						;------------------------------------------------------------------
593						; EXTRA SPRITES recovered from the tape that the original 47-block
594						; table did not cover:
595						;   ship banking phases $30-$32  (ship vanished at these bank angles)
596						;   saucer shot $4A  (its 'over the tower' shot shape)
597						;   satellite $92
598						;   mother ship $B2/$B3/$CA  (enemies were invisible without these)
599						; Packed at $8400 (bank-1 free RAM; relocated from $54C0 so the TOWER_TOP
600						; $53-$57 / TOWER_BOT $58-$5F sprites can occupy $54C0-$57C0); copied by
601						; INIT_SPRITES2 (called right after INIT_SPRITES). Bank-1 free RAM keeps this
602						; source intact across GAME OVER -> play-again restarts (a VIC-bank location
603						; would be overwritten at runtime, corrupting the ship bank frames $30-$32).
604						; None of these dests touch the HUD charset $4000-$40BF.
605						;------------------------------------------------------------------

1746						;------------------------------------------------------------------
1747						; RESET SPRITE SHADOW STATE FOR A FRESH GAME.
1748						; COLD_START only clears the $D000/$D400 hardware registers; the zero-page
1749						; sprite shadows (shot, ship phase, orbit-object arrays) otherwise survive a
1750						; GAME OVER -> play-again restart, so the IRQ would write stale pointers into
1751						; the hardware sprite table (corrupted ship / missile showing a "gate" sprite).
1752						; Called from INIT_GAME. Lives in free RAM (not the $0860 code region).
1753						;------------------------------------------------------------------
